# Aspen determinacy numbers — receipt registry

Confirmation keys use ACD_; every development quantity uses ACD_DEV_. Full precision is retained in JSON. Empirical paths refer to receipts/acd_stage2.json; development to receipts/acd_stage1.json. Derived quantities list actual input paths and the operation. Contract constants have separately identified sources under R-other; no nonexistent numeric receipt path is asserted.
